Interim Report 2026 中期報告
CORPORATE PROFILE
China Mengniu Dairy Company Limited (the “Company”; stock code: 2319) and its
subsidiaries (collectively “the Group” or “Mengniu”) mainly manufacture and distribute
quality dairy products in China. It is one of the leading dairy product manufacturers in
China, with MENGNIU as its core brand. Mengniu offers diversified products including
liquid milk products, ice cream, milk formula and cheese. In March 2014, Mengniu
became a Hang Seng Index constituent, making it the first blue-chip Chinese dairy
product manufacturer. Mengniu maintained its MSCI ESG Rating of “AA”, which is also
the highest rating received by comprehensive dairy companies in China. Mengniu is a
constituent of the Hang Seng Corporate Sustainability Index, Hang Seng (Mainland and
Hong Kong) Corporate Sustainability Index, Hang Seng Corporate Sustainability
Benchmark Index and HSI ESG Enhanced Select Index.




                                  公司簡介
                                  中國蒙牛乳業有限公司（ 「本公司」；股份代號：2319）及其子公司（統稱「本
                                  集團」或「蒙牛」）主要於中國生產及銷售優質乳製品。憑藉其主要品牌蒙牛 ，
                                  蒙牛已成為中國領先的乳製品生產商之一。蒙牛提供多元化的產品，包括
                                  液態奶、冰淇淋、奶粉及奶酪產品。二零一四年三月，蒙牛獲納入恒生指
                                  數成份股，成為首家中國乳製品企業藍籌股。蒙牛保持MSCI ESG評級AA
                                  級，是中國綜合型乳企獲得的最高評級，並入選恒生可持續發展企業指數、
                                  恒生內地及香港可持續發展企業指數、恒生可持續發展企業基準指數及恒
                                  指ESG增強精選指數。

      MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS
      管理層討論及分析




      Room Temperature Business                                                 常溫業務
      In the first half of 2026, the room temperature liquid milk industry      二零二六年上半年，常溫液態奶行業延續調整態
      remained in an adjustment phase and faced overlapping                     勢，仍面臨「需求分流、價格承壓、渠道重構」的
      challenges from “demand fragmentation, price pressure and                 疊加挑戰；與此同時，消費者對高品質、高性價
      channel restructuring”. At the same time, consumer demand                 比、精準營養及多元消費體驗的需求持續升級，
      continued to upgrade towards higher quality, better value for             會員倉儲店、零食量販、內容電商及即時零售等
      money, precision nutrition and more diverse consumption                   新興渠道快速發展，為品類轉型升級帶來結構性
      experiences. Emerging channels such as membership warehouse               機 遇。常 溫 事 業 部 圍 繞「喝 上 奶、喝 好 奶、喝 對
      stores, bulk snack retailers, content e-commerce and instant retail       奶」三層戰略框架及「普惠性價比、營養品質化、
      developed rapidly, creating structural opportunities for category         飲品街飲化、飲品清爽化、飲食功能化」五大戰
      transformation and upgrades. Guided by the three-tier strategic           略方向，推進產品創新、品牌建設、渠道精耕與
      framework of “Drink More, Drink Good and Drink Right” and the             經營提效，推動業務高質量發展。
      five strategic directions of “affordable value-for-money,
      quality-oriented nutrition, street-style ready-to-drink, refreshing and
      light, and functional food and beverage”, the room temperature
      liquid milk business unit continued to advance product innovation,
      brand building, channel cultivation and operational efficiency, to
      drive high-quality business growth.

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE                                                  企業管治
The Company is dedicated to ensuring high standards of                本公司致力確保企業管治達致高水平，尤其著重
corporate governance with an emphasis on a diligent Board,            組建勤勉盡職的董事會和健全的內部監控，以及
sound internal control, and increasing transparency and               提高透明度和對股東之問責性。董事會知悉，良
accountability to shareholders. The Board acknowledges that           好企業管治常規及程序對本集團及其股東有利，
good corporate governance practices and procedures are                有效的企業管治框架是促進及保障股東及其他利
beneficial to the Group and its shareholders, and that an effective   益相關者權益與提升股東價值的基本要素。
corporate governance framework is fundamental to promoting and
safeguarding the interests of Shareholders and other stakeholders
and enhancing Shareholder value.

Mengniu strives to create sustainable corporate governance by         蒙牛通過將ESG理念融入企業管理，制定GREEN
integrating ESG concepts into its corporate management, setting       戰略目標，致力創建可持續的企業管治。董事會
GREEN strategy goals. The Board believes that strong corporate        認為，強而有力的企業管治能為可持續增長及長
governance provides a solid foundation for sustainable growth and     遠成功奠定穩固基石。
long-term success.

enhance the business ethics management standards, and actively

SCOPE OF REVIEW                                                        審閱範圍
We conducted our review in accordance with Hong Kong                   我們是按照香港會計師公會頒佈的香港審閱委聘
Standard on Review Engagements 2410, Review of interim                 準則第2410號「由實體的獨立核數師執行的中期
financial information performed by the independent auditor of the      財務資料審閱」的規定進行審閱。中期財務報告
entity as issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public        的審閱主要包括向負責財務會計事宜的人士作出
Accountants. A review of interim financial report consists of          詢問，並實施分析性及其他審閱程序。審閱的範
making inquiries, primarily of persons responsible for financial and   圍遠較根據《香港審計準則》進行的審核範圍小，
accounting matters, and applying analytical and other review           故我們不能保證我們知悉一切可能於審核中識別
procedures. A review is substantially less in scope than an audit      的重大事宜。因此，我們不發表審核意見。
conducted in accordance with Hong Kong Standards on Auditing
and consequently does not enable us to obtain assurance that
we would become aware of all significant matters that might be
identified in an audit. Accordingly we do not express an audit
opinion.

30 June 2026 is not prepared, in all material respects, in             務報告在所有重大方面並未按照國際會計準則第

           statements.

           The Group expects that the adoption of IFRS/HKFRS 18                本集團預期採納國際財務報告準則╱香港
           will not change the Group’s net assets or net profit.               財務報告準則第18號不會改變本集團之淨
           However, it is expected to affect the presentation of the           資產或淨利潤。然而，預期其將影響本集團
           Group’s consolidated statement of profit or loss and related        綜合損益表之呈列方式及相關附註披露。
           note disclosures. The assessment remains subject to                 該評估仍可能因本集團持續實施工作而有
           change as the Group continues its implementation work.              所變動。

           (a)   Structure of the statement of profit or loss                  (a)   損益表結構

                 and expenses into specified categories in the                       則第18號要求實體將收益及開支於損
                 statement of profit or loss, including operating,                   益表中分類為指定類別，包括經營、
                 investing, and financing categories. It also requires               投資及融資類別。該準則亦要求實體
                 entities to present two newly defined subtotals, which              呈列兩項新訂義的小計項目，即「經
